Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the statistical_mechanics space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 612 and the convergence rate is O(176/n^1).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the statistical_mechanics decomposition. By minimality, the element a^612 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 176-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
